<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01MFNCoRukfvJqrnGKGcbTHe`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:intro}
3	
4	Consider estimating whether a content moderation policy reduces hate speech differently across demographic groups, using GPT-4 to classify thousands of posts.
5	After standard confusion-matrix correction, the analysis concludes the policy \emph{increases} hate speech among younger users---but the true effect is protective.
6	This sign reversal is not an edge case.
7	
8	Large language models (LLMs) now routinely replace human annotators in computational social science (CSS), producing labels for sentiment, hate speech, stance, and political discourse at costs orders of magnitude below manual coding~\cite{gilardi2023chatgpt,tornberg2024chatgpt,ziems2024nlp}.
9	When these labels serve as effect modifiers in heterogeneous treatment effect (HTE) analysis, the standard correction protocol\footnote{Throughout, ``debiasing'' refers to correction of annotation measurement-error bias, not algorithmic fairness debiasing.} is straightforward: validate a sample against expert annotations, estimate a confusion matrix, and apply its inverse uniformly to correct conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimates.
10	Yet LLM annotations are not uniformly accurate: \citet{ashwin2025bias} document misclassification rates varying by up to 5$\times$ across demographic subgroups, consistent with $\Delta\pi > 0.20$ at typical base error rates---well above the $0.15$ threshold at which EC-HTE becomes effective; in our pilot study, GPT-4.1 achieves 85.8\% accuracy on TweetEval sentiment, but error rates range from 10.2\% to 20.0\% across text-feature subgroups ($\Delta\pi = 0.098$).
11	
12	We show that this standard correction can make things worse.
13	In 14.9\% of our synthetic configurations (across a grid of $\Delta\pi$ and $\tau(0)$ values; $\tau(0) = 0.03$), global correction reverses the sign of estimated CATE---a protective treatment ($\tau = +0.03$) appears harmful ($\hat{\tau} = -0.059$)---rising to 31\% at $\tau(0) = 0.01$ (Figure~\ref{fig:sign_reversal}); all eight real LLMs tested amplify bias under global correction, while EC-HTE eliminates sign reversal entirely.
14	Global correction amplifies bias by up to 5.9$\times$ ($K{=}4$ with CausalForest); the classical Frazis-Loeb attenuation correction~\cite{frazis2003estimating} \emph{increases} bias by up to 2.9$\times$.
15	The mechanism is over-correction: pooled correction calibrated for the average error rate introduces substantial bias in low-error subgroups where the naive estimator is nearly unbiased.
16	
17	We derive an exact bias identity explaining when and why this occurs (Theorem~\ref{thm:bias_identity}): the correction-induced bias in each subgroup factors into three independently estimable components, verified at $R^2 \geq 0.97$ on synthetic grids and 11 empirical LLM confusion matrices.
18	The identity yields a diagnostic (Proposition~\ref{prop:debiasing_hurts}) that predicts when global correction will harm a subgroup using only per-subgroup misclassification rates, without estimating any treatment effects (AUC $= 1.0$ on subgroups with non-zero effect heterogeneity; Section~\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
19	
20	The per-subgroup correction prescribed by the identity---implemented via hierarchical Bayesian confusion matrix estimation (EC-HTE)---reduces $|\text{bias}|$ from 0.059 to 0.008 under extreme heterogeneity ($K{=}2$).
21	The advantage is conditional: when $\Delta\pi$ is small, the variance cost of stratification outweighs its bias benefit.
22	
23	Existing corrections---PPI~\cite{angelopoulos2023ppi}, DSL~\cite{egami2024dsl}, PPCI~\cite{sui2026ppci}, arm-specific PPI~\cite{mozer2026ppi}, ForestIV~\cite{bu2023forestiv}---assume homogeneous prediction error; none addresses effect modifier misclassification (Section~\ref{sec:related}).
24	
25	\begin{figure*}[t]
26	\centering
27	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sign_reversal.pdf}
28	\caption{CATE sign reversal under global correction ($K{=}2$, $\tau(0) = 0.03$). (A) Naive vs.\ global-corrected CATE; red triangles mark sign reversals (7.5\% of subgroup estimates). (B) Extreme case: true $\tau = +0.03$, estimated $\hat{\tau} = -0.059$. (C) 14.9\% of configurations exhibit sign reversal; all 8 real LLMs amplify bias; EC-HTE: 0\% reversal.}
29	\label{fig:sign_reversal}
30	\end{figure*}
31	
32	Our contributions are:
33	\begin{itemize}
34	    \item An exact finite-population bias identity (Theorem~\ref{thm:bias_identity}): the bias factors into three independently estimable components ($\delta_s \cdot \Delta\tau / (1-2\bar{\pi})$), explaining sign reversal in the LLM annotation setting and enabling the diagnostic below; verified at $R^2 \geq 0.97$ (Sections~\ref{sec:theory},~\ref{sec:bias_mechanism}).
35	    \item An actionable diagnostic (Proposition~\ref{prop:debiasing_hurts}): a condition on per-subgroup misclassification rates alone predicts when global correction increases bias, without estimating any treatment effects (AUC $= 1.0$ on subgroups with non-zero effect heterogeneity)---independently useful without adopting any specific correction method (Section~\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
36	    \item One corrective instantiation of the identity: per-subgroup hierarchical Bayesian confusion matrix estimation (EC-HTE), compatible with difference-in-means, CausalForest, and R-learner, and composable with PPCI for joint covariate/outcome error---effective when error heterogeneity is sufficient (Sections~\ref{sec:method}--\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
37	\end{itemize}
38	The identity and diagnostic (contributions 1--2) are the primary results, useful without adopting any correction method; EC-HTE is one instantiation showing the identity prescribes effective correction.
39	
```

fc=E_CORE, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB, lw=1.3, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\nrbox(X_COR, Y_EC, BW, EH, 'Per-subgroup Corr.',\n     r'$\\tilde{\\mu}_s = \\hat{C}_s^{-1} \\cdot \\hat{\\mu}_s$',\n     fc=E_CORE, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB, lw=1.3, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\n# Output box\nrbox(X_INF, Y_EC, BW, EH, 'Corrected CATE',\n     r'$\\hat{\\tau}(x) \\pm \\mathrm{SE}_{\\mathrm{corr}}$',\n     fc=G_BG, ec=G_EDGE, lc=G_TXT, sc='#66BB6A', fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\n# ✓ badge (hand-drawn checkmark, inside box corner)\nbx2 = X_INF + BW/2 - 0.25\nby2 = Y_EC + EH/2 - 0.13\nax.plot([bx2 - 0.12, bx2 - 0.02, bx2 + 0.13],\n        [by2 + 0.01, by2 - 0.12, by2 + 0.13],\n        color='#4CAF50', lw=2.5,\n        solid_capstyle='round', solid_joinstyle='round', zorder=5)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE ARROWS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\narrow(X_IN + BW_IN/2, Y_IT, X_EST - BW/2, Y_EC + 0.18,\n      c=E_ARR, rad=0.08)\narrow(X_IN + BW_IN/2, Y_IB, X_EST - BW/2, Y_EC - 0.18,\n      c=E_ARR, rad=-0.08)\n\narrow(X_EST + BW/2, Y_EC, X_COR - BW/2, Y_EC, c=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\narrow(X_COR + BW/2, Y_EC, X_INF - BW/2, Y_EC, c=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# CROSS-TRACK PRIOR ARROW (subtle)\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\narrow(X_EST, Y_STD - SH/2, X_EST, Y_EC + EH/2,\n      c=E_ARR, lw=0.7, ls='--', alpha=0.35, zz=1)\n\nax.text(X_EST + 0.22, 3.95, 'prior',\n        fontsize=7.5, color=E_ARR, fontstyle='italic',\n        alpha=0.55, ha='left', zorder=1)\n\n\n#    # Text\n    fw = 'bold' if bold else 'normal'\n    if sub:\n        ax.text(cx, cy + 0.16, label, ha='center', va='center',\n                fontsize=fs, fontweight=fw, color=lc, zorder=zz + 1)\n        ax.text(cx, cy - 0.20, sub, ha='center', va='center',\n                fontsize=ss, color=sc, fontstyle='italic', zorder=zz + 1)\n    else:\n        ax.text(cx, cy, label, ha='center', va='center',\n                fontsize=fs, fontweight=fw, color=lc, zorder=zz + 1)\n\n\ndef draw_arrow(x1, y1, x2, y2, color='gray', lw=1.0, rad=0,\n               ls='-', alpha=1.0, zz=2):\n solid_capstyle='round', zorder=zz)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# SWIM LANE BACKGROUNDS\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# Standard lane\nax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(\n    (0.3, Y_STD - BH_S / 2 - 0.35), 14.9, BH_S + 0.70,\n    boxstyle=f'round,pad=0,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',\n    fc=S_BG, ec='none', zorder=0, alpha=0.7))\n\n# alpha=0.7))\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# COLUMN HEADERS\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nfor label, x in [('Input', COL_X['input']),\n                  ('Estimation', COL_X['est']),\n                  ('Correction', COL_X['corr']),\n                  ('Inference', COL_X['inf'])]:\n    ax.text(x, Y_HEAD, label, ha='center', va='center',\n            fontsize=10.5, fontweight='bold', color=HEAD)\n\n# Thin divider under headers\nax.plot([0.5, 14.8], [Y_HEAD - 0.35, Y_HEAD - 0.35],\n        color=DIV, lw=0.6, zorder=0)\n\n# Vertical column dividers (very subtle)\nfor x in [3.9, 7.65, 11.35]:\n    ax.plot([x, x], [Y_HEAD - 0.35, Y_EC - BH_E / 2 - 0.55],\n            color=DIV, lw=0.3, ls=':', alpha=0.4, zorder=0)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# TRACK LABELS (left margin)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nax.text(0.50, Y_STD + BH_S / 2 + 0.15, 'Standard',\n        ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold',\n        color=S_TXT, alpha=0.8)\n\nax.text(0.50, Y_EC + BH_E / 2 + 0.35, 'EC-HTE (Ours)',\n        ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold',\n        color=E_TXT)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# STANDARD TRACK — 3 boxes\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ndraw_box(COL_X['est'], Y_STD, BW, BH_S,\n         r'Pooled CM  $\\hat{C}$', 'One matrix for all',\n         fc=S_BOX, ec=S_EDGE, lc=S_TXT, sc=S_SUB)\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['corr'], Y_STD, BW, BH_S,\n         'Global Correction', COL_X['inf'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_STD,\n           color=S_ARR, lw=1.0)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE TRACK — input + 3 core boxes\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# Input boxes (stacked vertically)\ny_in_top = Y_EC + 0.50\ny_in_bot = Y_EC - 0.50\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['input'], y_in_top, BW_IN, BH_I,\n         'LLM 'Expert Labels', r'$n_{\\mathrm{expert}}$ pairs $(Z, \\hat{Z})$',\n         fc=I_BOX, ec=I_EDGE, lc=I_TXT, sc=I_SUB,\n         fs=8.5, ss=6.5, bold=True)\n\n# Core boxes (slightly thicker borders for ss=7.5)\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['corr'], Y_EC, BW, BH_E,\n         'Per-subgroup Corr.', r'$\\tilde{\\mu}_s = \\hat{C}_s^{-1} \\cdot \\hat{\\mu}_s$',\n         fc=E_BOX, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB,\n ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE ARROWS\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# Input → Estimation (two converging arrows)\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['input'] + BW_IN / 2 + 0.05, ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# CROSS-TRACK \"prior\" ARROW (subtle dashed)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['est'], Y_STD - BH_S / 2 - 0.08,\n EC-HTE lane\nax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(\n    (0.3, Y_EC - BH_E / 2 - 0.55), 14.9, BH_E + 1.10,\n    boxstyle=f'round,pad=0,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',\n    fc=E_BG, ec='none', zorder=0, alpha=0.7))\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# COLUMN HEADERS\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nfor label, x in [('Input', COL_X['input']),\n                  ('Estimation', COL_X['est']),\n                  ('Correction', COL_X['corr']),\n                  ('Inference', COL_X['inf'])]:\n    ax.text(x, Y_HEAD, label, ha='center', va='center',\n            fontsize=10.5, fontweight='bold', color=HEAD)\n\n# Thin divider under headers\nax.plot([0.5, 14.8], [Y_HEAD - 0.35, Y_HEAD - 0.35],\n        color=DIV, lw=0.6, zorder=0)\n\n# Vertical column dividers (very subtle)\nfor x in [3.9, 7.65, 11.35]:\n    ax.plot([x, x], [Y_HEAD - 0.35, Y_EC - BH_E / 2 - 0.55],\n            color=DIV, lw=0.3, ls=':', alpha=0.4, zorder=0)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# TRACK LABELS (left margin)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nax.text(0.50, Y_STD + BH_S / 2 + 0.15, 'Standard',\n        ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold',\n        color=S_TXT, alpha=0.8)\n\nax.text(0.50, Y_EC + BH_E / 2 + 0.35, 'EC-HTE (Ours)',\n        ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold',\n        color=E_TXT)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# STANDARD TRACK — 3 boxes\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ndraw_box(COL_X['est'], Y_STD, BW, BH_S,\n         r'Pooled CM  $\\hat{C}$', 'One matrix for all',\n         fc=S_BOX, ec=S_EDGE, lc=S_TXT, sc=S_SUB)\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['corr'], Y_STD, BW, BH_S,\n         'Global Correction', r'$\\hat{C}^{-1}$ applied uniformly',\n         fc=S_BOX, ec=S_EDGE, lc=S_TXT, sc=S_SUB)\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['inf'], Y_STD, BW, BH_S,\n         'Sign Reversal', '14.9% of configs',\n         fc=BAD_BG, ec=BAD_EDGE, lc=BAD_TXT, sc='#D07070', lw=1.0)\n\n# ✗ badge on Sign Reversal box\ndraw_badge(COL_X['inf'] + BW / 2 - 0.28, Y_STD + BH_S / 2 - 0.18,\n           kind='cross')\n\n# Standard track arrows\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['est'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_STD,\n           COL_X['corr'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_STD,\n           color=S_ARR, lw=1.0)\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['corr'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_STD,\n           COL_X['inf'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_STD,\n           color=S_ARR, lw=1.0)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE TRACK — input + 3 core boxes\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# Input boxes (stacked vertically)\ny_in_top = Y_EC + 0.50\ny_in_bot = Y_EC - 0.50\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['input'], y_in_top, BW_IN, BH_I,\n         'LLM Annotations', r'$N$ noisy labels $\\hat{Z}$',\n         fc=I_BOX, ec=I_EDGE, lc=I_TXT, sc=I_SUB,\n         fs=8.5, ss=6.5, bold=True)\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['input'], y_in_bot, BW_IN, BH_I,\n         'Expert Labels', r'$n_{\\mathrm{expert}}$ pairs $(Z, \\hat{Z})$',\n         fc=I_BOX, ec=I_EDGE, lc=I_TXT, sc=I_SUB,\n         fs=8.5, ss=6.5, bold=True)\n\n# Core boxes (slightly thicker borders for emphasis)\ndraw_box(COL_X['est'], Y_EC, BW, BH_E,\n         'Hierarchical Bayes', r'Per-subgroup CMs $\\{\\hat{C}_s\\}$',\n         fc=E_BOX, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB,\n         lw=1.2, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['corr'], Y_EC, BW, BH_E,\n         'Per-subgroup Corr.', r'$\\tilde{\\mu}_s = \\hat{C}_s^{-1} \\cdot \\hat{\\mu}_s$',\n         fc=E_BOX, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB,\n         lw=1.2, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\ndraw_box(COL_X['inf'], Y_EC, BW, BH_E,\n         'Corrected CATE', r'$\\hat{\\tau}(x) \\pm \\mathrm{SE}_{\\mathrm{corr}}$',\n         fc=OK_BG, ec=OK_EDGE, lc=OK_TXT, sc='#5DA05D',\n         lw=1.2, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\n# ✓ badge on Corrected CATE box\ndraw_badge(COL_X['inf'] + BW / 2 - 0.28, Y_EC + BH_E / 2 - 0.18,\n           kind='check')\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE ARROWS\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# Input → Estimation (two converging arrows)\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['input'] + BW_IN / 2 + 0.05, y_in_top,\n           COL_X['est'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC + 0.15,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.0, rad=0.06)\n\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['input'] + BW_IN / 2 + 0.05, y_in_bot,\n           COL_X['est'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC - 0.15,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.0, rad=-0.06)\n\n# Estimation → Correction → Inference\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['est'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_EC,\n           COL_X['corr'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\n\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['corr'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_EC,\n           COL_X['inf'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# CROSS-TRACK \"prior\" ARROW (subtle dashed)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['est'], Y_STD - BH_S / 2 - 0.08,\n           COL_X['est'], Y_EC + BH_E / 2 + 0.08,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=0.7, ls='--', alpha=0.35, zz=1)\n\nax.text(COL_X['est'] + 0.25, (Y_STD - BH_S / 2 + Y_EC + BH_E / 2) / 2,\n        'prior', fontsize=7.5, color=E_ARR, fontstyle='italic',\n        alpha=0.55, ha='left', va='center', zorder=1)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# \"vs.\" DIVIDER\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ny_vs = (Y_STD - BH_S / 2 + Y_EC + BH_E / 2) / 2\nax.text(7.7, y_vs, 'vs.', ha='center', va='center',\n        fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', color='#C8C8C8',\n        zorder=1)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# ANNOTATIONS (below EC-HTE boxes)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nannot_y = Y_EC - BH_E / 2 - 0.22\n\nax.text(COL_X['est'], annot_y,\n        r'Partial pooling · $\\alpha_0$ via Emp. Bayes',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT,\n        fontstyle='italic')\n\nax.text(COL_X['corr'], annot_y,\n        r'$\\kappa$-guard: $\\kappa > 100$ → fallback',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT,\n        fontstyle='italic')\n\nax.text(COL_X['inf'], annot_y,\n        'via CausalForestDML',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=E_TXT,\n        fontstyle='italic')\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# RESULT HIGHLIGHT (bottom center)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nax.text(7.7, 0.15,\n        r'$\\mathbf{0\\%}$ sign reversal  ·  $|\\mathrm{bias}| \\div 7.4$',\n        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=8, color=OK_TXT,\n        fontweight='bold')\n\n# Diagnostic callout (boxed)\nax.text(7.7, -0.45,\n        r'Diagnostic (Prop. 2):  $|\\delta_s| \\,/\\, ((1{-}2\\bar{\\pi}) \\cdot \\pi_s) > 1$',\n        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=7, color='#E65100',\n        fontweight='bold',\n        bbox=dict(boxstyle=f'round,pad=0.22,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',\n                  fc='#FFF8E1', ec='#FFB74D', lw=0.6, alpha=0.9))\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# SAVE\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.png', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.pdf', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nplt.close()\nprint('Saved fig_method_overview v20')\n",

          "+def draw_box(cx, cy, w, h, label, sub='',",
          "+             fc='white', ec='gray', lw=0.8,",
          "+             fs=9.5, ss=7, lc='#333', sc='#777',",
          "+             bold=True, shadow=True, zz=3):",
          "+    \"\"\"Draw a rounded box with optional shadow and two-line label.\"\"\"",
          "+    x0, y0 = cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2",

          "+            fc=SHADOW_COLOR, ec='none', zorder=zz - 1))",

          "         fc=fc, ec=ec, lw=lw, zorder=zz))",

          "+    # Text",

          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-ax.text(0.55, Y_STD + 0.65, 'Standard', ha='left', va='center',",
          "-        fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold', color=S_TXT, alpha=0.85)",
          " ",
          "-ax.text(0.55, 3.25, 'EC-HTE (Ours)', ha='left', va='center',",
          "-        fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold', color=E_TXT)",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# TRACK LABELS (left margin)",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+ax.text(0.50, Y_STD + BH_S / 2 + 0.15, 'Standard',",
          "+        ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold',",
          "+        color=S_TXT, alpha=0.8)",
          " ",
          "+ax.text(0.50, Y_EC + BH_E / 2 + 0.35, 'EC-HTE (Ours)',",
          "+        ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold',",
          "+        color=E_TXT)",
          " ",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-# STANDARD TRACK (3 boxes)",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-rbox(X_EST, Y_STD, BW, SH, r'Pooled CM  $\\hat{C}$',",

          "-ax.plot([bx-d, bx+d], [by+d, by-d], color='#E53935', lw=2.5,",

          "-arrow(X_COR + BW/2, Y_STD, X_INF - BW/2, Y_STD, c=S_ARR)",

          "+draw_badge(COL_X['inf'] + BW / 2 - 0.28, Y_STD + BH_S / 2 - 0.18,",

          "+draw_arrow(COL_X['est'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_STD,",
          "+           COL_X['corr'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_STD,",

          "+draw_arrow(COL_X['corr'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_STD,",
          "+           COL_X['inf'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_STD,",
          "+           color=S_ARR, lw=1.0)",
          " ",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-# EC-HTE TRACK",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-# Input boxes",
          "-rbox(X_IN, Y_IT, BW_IN, IH, 'LLM Annotations',",
          "-     r'$N$ noisy labels $\\hat{Z}$',",
          "-     fc=I_BG, ec=I_EDGE, lc=I_TXT, sc=I_SUB, fs=8.5, ss=6.5)",
          " ",
          "-rbox(X_IN, Y_IB, BW_IN, IH, 'Expert Labels',",
          "-     r'$n_{\\mathrm{expert}}$ pairs $(Z, \\hat{Z})$',",

          "+# EC-HTE TRACK — input + 3 core boxes",

          "+y_in_top = Y_EC + 0.50",
          "+y_in_bot = Y_EC - 0.50",
          " ",
          "-# Core boxes (thicker borders = emphasis)",

          "-     fc=E_CORE, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB, lw=1.3, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)",

          "+draw_box(COL_X['input'], y_in_bot, BW_IN, BH_I,",

          "+draw_box(COL_X['corr'], Y_EC, BW, BH_E,",
          "+         'Per-subgroup Corr.', r'$\\tilde{\\mu}_s = \\hat{C}_s^{-1} \\cdot \\hat{\\mu}_s$',",
          "+         fc=E_BOX, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB,",
          "+         lw=1.2, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)",
          " ",
          "+draw_box(COL_X['inf'], Y_EC, BW, BH_E,",
          "+         'Corrected CATE', r'$\\hat{\\tau}(x) \\pm \\mathrm{SE}_{\\mathrm{corr}}$',",
          "+         fc=OK_BG, ec=OK_EDGE, lc=OK_TXT, sc='#5DA05D',",
          "+         lw=1.2, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)",
          " ",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# ✓ badge on Corrected CATE box",
          "+draw_badge(COL_X['inf'] + BW / 2 - 0.28, Y_EC + BH_E / 2 - 0.18,",
          "+           kind='check')",
          "+",
          "+",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          " # EC-HTE ARROWS",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-arrow(X_IN + BW_IN/2, Y_IT, X_EST - BW/2, Y_EC + 0.18,",
          "-      c=E_ARR, rad=0.08)",
          "-arrow(X_IN + BW_IN/2, Y_IB, X_EST - BW/2, Y_EC - 0.18,",
          "-      c=E_ARR, rad=-0.08)",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# Input → Estimation (two converging arrows)",
          "+draw_arrow(COL_X['input'] + BW_IN / 2 + 0.05, y_in_top,",
          "+           COL_X['est'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC + 0.15,",
          "+           color=E_ARR, lw=1.0, rad=0.06)",
          " ",
          "-arrow(X_EST + BW/2, Y_EC, X_COR - BW/2, Y_EC, c=E_ARR, lw=1.3)",
          "-arrow(X_COR + BW/2, Y_EC, X_INF - BW/2, Y_EC, c=E_ARR, lw=1.3)",
          "+draw_arrow(COL_X['input'] + BW_IN / 2 + 0.05, y_in_bot,",
          "+           COL_X['est'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC - 0.15,",
          "+           color=E_ARR, lw=1.0, rad=-0.06)",
          " ",
          "+# Estimation → Correction → Inference",
          "+draw_arrow(COL_X['est'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_EC,",
          "+           COL_X['corr'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC,",
          "+           color=E_ARR, lw=1.3)",
          " ",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-# CROSS-TRACK PRIOR ARROW (subtle)",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-arrow(X_EST, Y_STD - SH/2, X_EST, Y_EC + EH/2,",
          "-      c=E_ARR, lw=0.7, ls='--', alpha=0.35, zz=1)",
          "+draw_arrow(COL_X['corr'] + BW / 2 + 0.05, Y_EC,",
          "+           COL_X['inf'] - BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC,",
          "+           color=E_ARR, lw=1.3)",
          " ",
          "-ax.text(X_EST + 0.22, 3.95, 'prior',",
          "-        fontsize=7.5, color=E_ARR, fontstyle='italic',",
          "-        alpha=0.55, ha='left', zorder=1)",
          " ",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# CROSS-TRACK \"prior\" ARROW (subtle dashed)",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+draw_arrow(COL_X['est'], Y_STD - BH_S / 2 - 0.08,",
          "+           COL_X['est'], Y_EC + BH_E / 2 + 0.08,",
          "+           color=E_ARR, lw=0.7, ls='--', alpha=0.35, zz=1)",
          " ",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "+ax.text(COL_X['est'] + 0.25, (Y_STD - BH_S / 2 + Y_EC + BH_E / 2) / 2,",
          "+        'prior', fontsize=7.5, color=E_ARR, fontstyle='italic',",
          "+        alpha=0.55, ha='left', va='center', zorder=1)",
          "+",
          "+",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          " # \"vs.\" DIVIDER",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-y_vs = (Y_STD - SH/2 + Y_EC + EH/2) / 2",
          "-ax.text(7.3, y_vs, 'vs.', ha='center', va='center',",
          "-        fontsize=11, fontweight='bold', color='#C0C0C0')",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+y_vs = (Y_STD - BH_S / 2 + Y_EC + BH_E / 2) / 2",
          "+ax.text(7.7, y_vs, 'vs.', ha='center', va='center',",
          "+        fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', color='#C8C8C8',",
          "+        zorder=1)",
          " ",
          " ",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-# ANNOTATIONS",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "-annot_y = Y_EC - EH/2 - 0.15",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# ANNOTATIONS (below EC-HTE boxes)",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+annot_y = Y_EC - BH_E / 2 - 0.22",
          " ",
          "-ax.text(X_EST, annot_y, r'Partial pooling · $\\alpha_0$ via Emp. Bayes',",
          "-        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT, fontstyle='italic')",
          "+ax.text(COL_X['est'], annot_y,",
          "+        r'Partial pooling · $\\alpha_0$ via Emp. Bayes',",
          "+        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT,",
          "+        fontstyle='italic')",
          " ",
          "-ax.text(X_COR, annot_y, r'$\\kappa$-guard: $\\kappa > 100$ $\\rightarrow$ fallback',",
          "-        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT, fontstyle='italic')",
          "+ax.text(COL_X['corr'], annot_y,",
          "+        r'$\\kappa$-guard: $\\kappa > 100$ → fallback',",
          "+        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT,",
          "+        fontstyle='italic')",
          " ",
          "-ax.text(X_INF, annot_y, 'via CausalForestDML',",
          "-        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=E_TXT, fontstyle='italic')",
          "+ax.text(COL_X['inf'], annot_y,",
          "+        'via CausalForestDML',",
          "+        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=E_TXT,",
          "+        fontstyle='italic')",
          " ",
          "-# Result highlight",
          "-ax.text(7.3, 0.25,",
          "+",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# RESULT HIGHLIGHT (bottom center)",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          "+ax.text(7.7, 0.15,",
          "         r'$\\mathbf{0\\%}$ sign reversal  ·  $|\\mathrm{bias}| \\div 7.4$',",
          "-        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=7.5, color=G_TXT, fontweight='bold')",
          "+        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=8, color=OK_TXT,",
          "+        fontweight='bold')",
          " ",
          "-# Diagnostic callout",
          "-ax.text(7.3, -0.25,",
          "+# Diagnostic callout (boxed)",
          "+ax.text(7.7, -0.45,",
          "         r'Diagnostic (Prop. 2):  $|\\delta_s| \\,/\\, ((1{-}2\\bar{\\pi}) \\cdot \\pi_s) > 1$',",
          "-        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=6.5, color='#E65100', fontweight='bold',",
          "-        bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.18,rounding_size=0.12',",
          "-                  fc='#FFF8E1', ec='#FFB74D', lw=0.6))",
          "+        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=7, color='#E65100',",
          "+        fontweight='bold',",
          "+        bbox=dict(boxstyle=f'round,pad=0.22,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',",
          "+                  fc='#FFF8E1', ec='#FFB74D', lw=0.6, alpha=0.9))",
          " ",
          " ",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          " # SAVE",
          "-# ═══════════════════════════════════════",
          "+# ══════════════════════════════════════════",
          " fig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.png', dpi=300, facecolor='white')",
          " fig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.pdf', dpi=300, facecolor='white')",

    "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Figure 2: EC-HTE Method Overview — v11 polished.\n\nTwo-track comparison: Standard (muted rose) vs EC-HTE (sage green).\nfigsize=(7.2, 3.8) → scales to ~6.3\" at \\\\textwidth.\n\"\"\"\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom matplotlib.patches import FancyBboxPatch, FancyArrowPatch\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'serif',\n    'font.size': 8,\n    'mathtext.fontset': 'stix',\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42,\n    'ps.fonttype': 42,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n})\n\nOUT = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0520/projects/ec_hte_llm_annotation/docs/paper/figures'\n\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 3.8))\nax.set_xlim(-0.2, 14.6)\nax.set_ylim(-0.7, 7.4)\nax.axis('off')\nfig.patch.set_facecolor('white')\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# PALETTE (3 color families only)\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nS_BG   = '#FDF3F1';  S_BOX  = '#FFF7F6';  S_EDGE = '#C98E87'\nS_TXT  = '#7A4440';  S_SUB  = '#B07872';  S_ARR  = '#D4AAA5'\n\nE_BG   = '#F2F8F2';  E_CORE = '#E4F2E4';  E_EDGE = '#6BAE6B'\nE_TXT  = '#2D5C2D';  E_SUB  = '#4A8A4A';  E_ARR  = '#6BAE6B'\n\nI_BG   = '#EDF4FB';  I_EDGE = '#8AB6D9'\nI_TXT  = '#2C5D89';  I_SUB  = '#5A8CB5'\n\nG_BG   = '#E8F5E9';  G_EDGE = '#66BB6A';  G_TXT  = '#2E7D32'\nB_BG   = '#FFEBEE';  B_EDGE = '#EF5350';  B_TXT  = '#C62828'\n\nHEAD = '#37474F';  ANNOT = '#8A8A8A';  DIV = '#E8E8E8'\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# LAYOUT CONSTANTS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nX_IN, X_EST, X_COR, X_INF = 2.0, 5.5, 9.0, 12.5\nBW_IN, BW = 2.1, 2.4\n\nY_HEAD = 6.8\nY_STD  = 5.2\nY_EC   = 2.1\nY_IT   = 2.7\nY_IB   = 1.5\n\nSH  = 0.85\nEH  = 1.0\nIH  = 0.65\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# DRAWING HELPERS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\ndef rbox(cx, cy, w, h, label, sub='',\n         fc='white', ec='gray', lw=0.9,\n         fs=9, ss=7, lc='#333', sc='#777', zz=3):\n    x0, y0 = cx - w/2, cy - h/2\n    ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(\n        (x0 + 0.05, y0 - 0.05), w, h,\n        boxstyle='round,pad=0.06,rounding_size=0.12',\n        fc='#0000000A', ec='none', zorder=zz - 1))\n    ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(\n        (x0, y0), w, h,\n        boxstyle='round,pad=0.06,rounding_size=0.12',\n        fc=fc, ec=ec, lw=lw, zorder=zz))\n    if sub:\n        ax.text(cx, cy + 0.15, label, ha='center', va='center',\n                fontsize=fs, fontweight='bold', color=lc, zorder=zz + 1)\n        ax.text(cx, cy - 0.17, sub, ha='center', va='center',\n                fontsize=ss, color=sc, fontstyle='italic', zorder=zz + 1)\n    else:\n        ax.text(cx, cy, label, ha='center', va='center',\n                fontsize=fs, fontweight='bold', color=lc, zorder=zz + 1)\n\n\ndef arrow(x1, y1, x2, y2, c='gray', lw=1.0, ms=9, rad=0,\n          ls='-', alpha=1.0, zz=2):\n    ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch(\n        (x1, y1), (x2, y2),\n        arrowstyle='->,head_length=4,head_width=2.2',\n        color=c, lw=lw, mutation_scale=ms,\n        connectionstyle=f'arc3,rad={rad}',\n        linestyle=ls, alpha=alpha, zorder=zz))\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# SWIM LANE BACKGROUNDS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(\n    (0.4, Y_STD - 0.6), 13.8, 1.2,\n    boxstyle='round,pad=0,rounding_size=0.14',\n    fc=S_BG, ec='none', zorder=0))\n\nax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(\n    (0.4, Y_EC - 1.0), 13.8, 2.0,\n    boxstyle='round,pad=0,rounding_size=0.14',\n    fc=E_BG, ec='none', zorder=0))\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# PHASE HEADERS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nfor label, x in [('Input', X_IN), ('Estimation', X_EST),\n                  ('Correction', X_COR), ('Inference', X_INF)]:\n    ax.text(x, Y_HEAD, label, ha='center', va='center',\n            fontsize=10, fontweight='bold', color=HEAD)\n\nax.plot([0.6, 14.0], [Y_HEAD - 0.3, Y_HEAD - 0.3],\n        color=DIV, lw=0.5, zorder=0)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# TRACK LABELS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nax.text(0.55, Y_STD + 0.65, 'Standard', ha='left', va='center',\n        fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold', color=S_TXT, alpha=0.85)\n\nax.text(0.55, 3.25, 'EC-HTE (Ours)', ha='left', va='center',\n        fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold', color=E_TXT)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# STANDARD TRACK (3 boxes)\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nrbox(X_EST, Y_STD, BW, SH, r'Pooled CM  $\\hat{C}$',\n     'One matrix for all',\n     fc=S_BOX, ec=S_EDGE, lc=S_TXT, sc=S_SUB)\n\nrbox(X_COR, Y_STD, BW, SH, 'Global Correction',\n     r'$\\hat{C}^{-1}$ applied uniformly',\n     fc=S_BOX, ec=S_EDGE, lc=S_TXT, sc=S_SUB)\n\nrbox(X_INF, Y_STD, BW, SH, 'Sign Reversal',\n     '14.9% of configs',\n     fc=B_BG, ec=B_EDGE, lc=B_TXT, sc='#D4837D')\n\n# ✗ badge (hand-drawn cross, inside box corner)\nbx, by = X_INF + BW/2 - 0.25, Y_STD + SH/2 - 0.13\nd = 0.13\nax.plot([bx-d, bx+d], [by-d, by+d], color='#E53935', lw=2.5,\n        solid_capstyle='round', zorder=5)\nax.plot([bx-d, bx+d], [by+d, by-d], color='#E53935', lw=2.5,\n        solid_capstyle='round', zorder=5)\n\narrow(X_EST + BW/2, Y_STD, X_COR - BW/2, Y_STD, c=S_ARR)\narrow(X_COR + BW/2, Y_STD, X_INF - BW/2, Y_STD, c=S_ARR)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE TRACK\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# Input boxes\nrbox(X_IN, Y_IT, BW_IN, IH, 'LLM Annotations',\n     r'$N$ noisy labels $\\hat{Z}$',\n     fc=I_BG, ec=I_EDGE, lc=I_TXT, sc=I_SUB, fs=8.5, ss=6.5)\n\nrbox(X_IN, Y_IB, BW_IN, IH, 'Expert Labels',\n     r'$n_{\\mathrm{expert}}$ pairs $(Z, \\hat{Z})$',\n     fc=I_BG, ec=I_EDGE, lc=I_TXT, sc=I_SUB, fs=8.5, ss=6.5)\n\n# Core boxes (thicker borders = emphasis)\nrbox(X_EST, Y_EC, BW, EH, 'Hierarchical Bayes',\n     r'Per-subgroup CMs $\\{\\hat{C}_s\\}$',\n     fc=E_CORE, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB, lw=1.3, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\nrbox(X_COR, Y_EC, BW, EH, 'Per-subgroup Corr.',\n     r'$\\tilde{\\mu}_s = \\hat{C}_s^{-1} \\cdot \\hat{\\mu}_s$',\n     fc=E_CORE, ec=E_EDGE, lc=E_TXT, sc=E_SUB, lw=1.3, fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\n# Output box\nrbox(X_INF, Y_EC, BW, EH, 'Corrected CATE',\n     r'$\\hat{\\tau}(x) \\pm \\mathrm{SE}_{\\mathrm{corr}}$',\n     fc=G_BG, ec=G_EDGE, lc=G_TXT, sc='#66BB6A', fs=9.5, ss=7.5)\n\n# ✓ badge (hand-drawn checkmark, inside box corner)\nbx2 = X_INF + BW/2 - 0.25\nby2 = Y_EC + EH/2 - 0.13\nax.plot([bx2 - 0.12, bx2 - 0.02, bx2 + 0.13],\n        [by2 + 0.01, by2 - 0.12, by2 + 0.13],\n        color='#4CAF50', lw=2.5,\n        solid_capstyle='round', solid_joinstyle='round', zorder=5)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE ARROWS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\narrow(X_IN + BW_IN/2, Y_IT, X_EST - BW/2, Y_EC + 0.18,\n      c=E_ARR, rad=0.08)\narrow(X_IN + BW_IN/2, Y_IB, X_EST - BW/2, Y_EC - 0.18,\n      c=E_ARR, rad=-0.08)\n\narrow(X_EST + BW/2, Y_EC, X_COR - BW/2, Y_EC, c=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\narrow(X_COR + BW/2, Y_EC, X_INF - BW/2, Y_EC, c=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# CROSS-TRACK PRIOR ARROW (subtle)\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\narrow(X_EST, Y_STD - SH/2, X_EST, Y_EC + EH/2,\n      c=E_ARR, lw=0.7, ls='--', alpha=0.35, zz=1)\n\nax.text(X_EST + 0.22, 3.95, 'prior',\n        fontsize=7.5, color=E_ARR, fontstyle='italic',\n        alpha=0.55, ha='left', zorder=1)\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# \"vs.\" DIVIDER\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\ny_vs = (Y_STD - SH/2 + Y_EC + EH/2) / 2\nax.text(7.3, y_vs, 'vs.', ha='center', va='center',\n        fontsize=11, fontweight='bold', color='#C0C0C0')\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# ANNOTATIONS\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nannot_y = Y_EC - EH/2 - 0.15\n\nax.text(X_EST, annot_y, r'Partial pooling · $\\alpha_0$ via Emp. Bayes',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT, fontstyle='italic')\n\nax.text(X_COR, annot_y, r'$\\kappa$-guard: $\\kappa > 100$ $\\rightarrow$ fallback',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=ANNOT, fontstyle='italic')\n\nax.text(X_INF, annot_y, 'via CausalForestDML',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=6.5, color=E_TXT, fontstyle='italic')\n\n# Result highlight\nax.text(7.3, 0.25,\n        r'$\\mathbf{0\\%}$ sign reversal  ·  $|\\mathrm{bias}| \\div 7.4$',\n        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=7.5, color=G_TXT, fontweight='bold')\n\n# Diagnostic callout\nax.text(7.3, -0.25,\n        r'Diagnostic (Prop. 2):  $|\\delta_s| \\,/\\, ((1{-}2\\bar{\\pi}) \\cdot \\pi_s) > 1$',\n        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=6.5, color='#E65100', fontweight='bold',\n        bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.18,rounding_size=0.12',\n                  fc='#FFF8E1', ec='#FFB74D', lw=0.6))\n\n\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\n# SAVE\n# ═══════════════════════════════════════\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.png', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.pdf', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nplt.close()\nprint('Done v12 (final)')\n",

  "new_string": "def draw_badge(cx, cy, kind='check', radius=0.22, zz=6):\n    \"\"\"Draw a ✓ or ✗ badge inside a small circle at top-right corner.\"\"\"\n    # Background circle\n    bg_color = '#E8F5E9' if kind == 'check' else '#FFEBEE'\n    edge_color = '#43A047' if kind == 'check' else '#E53935'\n    circle = plt.Circle((cx, cy), radius, fc=bg_color, ec=edge_color,\n                         lw=1.0, zorder=zz, clip_on=False)\n    ax.add_patch(circle)\n\n    s = radius * 0.55\n    if kind == 'check':\n        ax.plot([cx - s * 0.7, cx - s * 0.05, cx + s],\n                [cy + s * 0.05, cy - s * 0.6, cy + s * 0.55],\n                color='#43A047', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',\n                solid_joinstyle='round', zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)\n    else:\n        d = s * 0.6\n        ax.plot([cx - d, cx + d], [cy - d, cy + d],\n                color='#E53935', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',\n                zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)\n        ax.plot([cx - d, cx + d], [cy + d, cy - d],\n                color='#E53935', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',\n                zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)"
}

          "-        d = size * 0.7",
          "+        ax.plot([cx - s * 0.7, cx - s * 0.05, cx + s],",
          "+                [cy + s * 0.05, cy - s * 0.6, cy + s * 0.55],",
          "+                color='#43A047', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',",
          "+                solid_joinstyle='round', zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)",
          "+    else:",
          "+        d = s * 0.6",
          "         ax.plot([cx - d, cx + d], [cy - d, cy + d],",
          "-                color='#E53935', lw=2.8, solid_capstyle='round', zorder=zz)",
          "+                color='#E53935', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',",
          "+                zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)",
          "         ax.plot([cx - d, cx + d], [cy + d, cy - d],",
          "-                color='#E53935', lw=2.8, solid_capstyle='round', zorder=zz)",
          "+                color='#E53935', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',",
          "+                zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)",
          " ",
          " ",
          " # ══════════════════════════════════════════"
        ]
      }

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The badges are now clean and not overlapping with text. The overall layout is much cleaner than the original. But let me do another round of critique:

1. The badges look good - circled ✗ and ✓ at top-right corners
2. The box spacing is improved
3. The color scheme works well (rose for standard, green for EC-HTE)
4. The "vs." divider is subtle and clean

Issues to fix in next iteration:
1. The "Input" column is empty for the Standard track - this is by design since both tracks share the same inputs, but visually it leaves a gap. 

I should restructure the standard track to start from the Estimation column instead, and make the input boxes wider for better readability. I'll also add a note clarifying that both tracks share the same input, and bump up the font sizes across the board to ensure everything's legible. The prior arrow and bottom annotations are working well, so I'll leave those as is.

Now I'm thinking through the figure dimensions for the EMNLP format—at 8 inches wide, it'll scale down to about 6.3 inches to fit the two-column layout, which means fonts will shrink by roughly 21%. That could push smaller annotations below the readability threshold, so I need to increase the annotation and subtitle font sizes preemptively to account for that scaling.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The figure looks much better now. The badges are clean and don't overlap text. The font sizes are good. Let me do a final round of critique:

Issues:
1. The "Hierarchical Bayes" and "Per-subgroup Corr." boxes appear to almost touch each other - the text from "Hierarchical Bayes" right edge is very close to "Per-subgroup Corr." left edge. But this might just be the thumbnail - let me check actual coordinates.
   - COL_X['est']=6.0, COL_X['corr']=10.0, BW=2.5
   - Right edge of est box: 6.0 + 1.25 = 7.25
   - Left edge of corr box: 10.0 - 1.25 = 8.75
   - Gap: 8.75 - 7.25 = 1.5 units → that's plenty of space. The boxes aren't touching, it's just the small thumbnail making it look that way.

2. The "prior" arrow and label between the tracks is nicely subtle
3. The badge circles look professional
4. The diagnostic callout at the bottom is clean and bounded
5. The result highlight is prominent

Now I'm verifying the standard track spacing too—the gaps between est and corr, and between corr and inf are both holding at 1.5 units, so the layout should feel balanced across all three. The real question is whether this reads as publication-quality work. Compared to where I started, this is a massive improvement: the spacing is clean, the boxes are consistent, there's no text collision, the badges feel polished, and the color scheme actually conveys meaning with proper visual hierarchy.

I think one more refinement pass would help—specifically making the input boxes a bit taller to give the text more breathing room with the larger fonts. The figure flows well left-to-right, the two tracks are visually distinct, and the diagnostic callout lands just right. This feels ready to move forward, so I'll do that final height adjustment and then shift focus to the remaining figures. I'll come back for a final review once the others are done.
```

```text
1	\section{EC-HTE Procedure}
2	\label{app:algorithm}
3	
4	\begin{algorithm}[t]
5	\caption{EC-HTE: Error-Corrected Heterogeneous Treatment Effects}
6	\label{alg:echte}
7	\begin{algorithmic}[1]
8	\Require Observations $\{(Y_i, W_i, \hat{Z}_i, X_i)\}_{i=1}^N$; expert labels $\{Z_j^*\}_{j=1}^{n_{\text{expert}}}$; subgroup map $S: \mathcal{X} \to \{1,\ldots,K\}$
9	\Ensure Corrected CATE estimates $\hat{\tau}(z)$ with variance-adjusted CIs
10	\Statex
11	\State \textbf{Preconditions:} verify $n_{\text{expert}} \times P(Z_{\text{minority}}) / K \geq 25$ and $\max_s(\text{FPR}_s, \text{FNR}_s) < 0.5$; if either fails, use global correction
12	\Statex
13	\State \textbf{Step 1: Estimate per-subgroup confusion matrices (HB)}
14	\For{$s = 1, \ldots, K$}
15	    \State $C_s[j,\cdot] \sim \mathrm{Dir}(\alpha_0 \cdot \hat{C}_{\text{global},j})$, \; $\alpha_0$ via empirical Bayes
16	    \State Gibbs sampler (4 chains, 4000 iter) on $\{(\hat{Z}_j, Z_j^*) : S_j = s\}$
17	    \State $\hat{C}_s \gets$ posterior mean; verify $\hat{R} < 1.01$
18	\EndFor
19	\Statex
20	\State \textbf{Step 2: Correct observed outcome moments}
21	\For{$s = 1, \ldots, K$}
22	    \If{$\kappa(\hat{C}_s) > 100$} $\hat{C}_s \gets \bar{C}$ \Comment{singularity guard}
23	    \EndIf
24	    \State $\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_s \gets \hat{C}_s^{-1} \hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_s$
25	\EndFor
26	\Statex
27	\State \textbf{Step 3: CausalForestDML with variance-adjusted SEs}
28	\State Train CausalForest (honest splitting) on corrected outcome moments $\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_s$
29	\State $\mathrm{se}_{\mathrm{adj},i} \gets \sqrt{\mathrm{diag}(\hat{C}_{S_i}^{-1}\, \mathrm{diag}(\mathrm{se}_i^2)\, \hat{C}_{S_i}^{-\top})}$
30	\State \Return $\hat{\tau}(z)$ with 95\% CIs from $\mathrm{se}_{\mathrm{adj}}$
31	\end{algorithmic}
32	\end{algorithm}
33	
34	\subsection{Condition Number Guard Ablation}
35	\label{app:kappa_ablation}
36	
37	The singularity guard in Algorithm~\ref{alg:echte} (line~6) falls back to global correction when $\kappa(\hat{C}_s) > 100$.
38	We verify that this threshold is not load-bearing by sweeping $\kappa \in \{10, 25, 50, 100, 200, 500, 1000\}$ under the standard DGP ($K{=}2$, extreme heterogeneity, $N{=}5{,}000$, $n_{\text{expert}}{=}250$, 100 MC runs).
39	Across all configurations, the maximum observed condition number is $\kappa_{\max} = 1.89$ (mean 1.45, p99 1.89), providing a $53{\times}$ safety margin below the $\kappa{=}100$ threshold.
40	Consequently, the guard never triggers at any tested threshold, and all $\kappa$ values produce identical RMSE (HB: 0.076, Global: 0.077) and zero numerical instability.
41	In a stress test with near-random labeling ($\pi_s \approx 0.4$--$0.5$, $n_{\text{expert}}{=}50$), the maximum condition number reaches 1{,}125, correctly triggering the $\kappa{=}100$ guard and preventing catastrophic inversion.
42	The adaptive formula $\kappa_{\text{adapt}} = 10 \sqrt{n_{\text{expert}} / K}$ recovers $\kappa{=}100$ for this regime and scales naturally with the estimation precision.
43	
44	\section{Full Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:bias_identity}}
45	\label{app:derivation}
46	
47	\paragraph{Setup.}
48	Under assumptions (A1)--(A4), the observed CATE in subgroup $s$ at effect-modifier level $z$ is
49	\begin{equation}
50	\tau_{\text{obs}}(z, s) = \sum_{z'} M_s[z, z'] \cdot \tau(z'),
51	\end{equation}
52	where $M_s[z, z'] = P(Z{=}z' \mid \hat{Z}{=}z, S{=}s)$ is the mixing matrix.
53	Under (A2)--(A3), $M_s = \bigl[\begin{smallmatrix} 1-\pi_s & \pi_s \\ \pi_s & 1-\pi_s \end{smallmatrix}\bigr]$.
54	
55	\paragraph{Global correction.}
56	The pooled mixing matrix is $M_{\text{global}} = \sum_s w_s M_s = \bigl[\begin{smallmatrix} 1-\bar{\pi} & \bar{\pi} \\ \bar{\pi} & 1-\bar{\pi} \end{smallmatrix}\bigr]$, with $\bar{\pi} = \sum_s w_s \pi_s$.
57	
58	Global correction applies $M_{\text{global}}^{-1}$ to $\tau_{\text{obs}}(\cdot, s)$:
59	\begin{equation}
60	\hat{\tau}_{\text{global}}(z, s) = [M_{\text{global}}^{-1} M_s \cdot \boldsymbol{\tau}]_z,
61	\end{equation}
62	where $\boldsymbol{\tau} = [\tau(0), \tau(1)]^\top$.
63	
64	\paragraph{Bias computation.}
65	The bias is $\hat{\tau}_{\text{global}}(z, s) - \tau(z) = [(M_{\text{global}}^{-1} M_s - I) \boldsymbol{\tau}]_z$.
66	
67	Direct computation gives:
68	\begin{equation}
69	M_{\text{global}}^{-1} = \frac{1}{1 - 2\bar{\pi}} \begin{bmatrix} 1-\bar{\pi} & -\bar{\pi} \\ -\bar{\pi} & 1-\bar{\pi} \end{bmatrix}.
70	\end{equation}
71	Multiplying by $M_s$:
72	\begin{equation}
73	\resizebox{.88\columnwidth}{!}{$\displaystyle
74	M_{\text{global}}^{-1} M_s = \frac{1}{1-2\bar{\pi}} \begin{bmatrix} (1{-}\bar{\pi})(1{-}\pi_s) - \bar{\pi}\pi_s & (1{-}\bar{\pi})\pi_s - \bar{\pi}(1{-}\pi_s) \\ -\bar{\pi}(1{-}\pi_s) + (1{-}\bar{\pi})\pi_s & -\bar{\pi}\pi_s + (1{-}\bar{\pi})(1{-}\pi_s) \end{bmatrix}.
75	$}
76	\end{equation}
77	
78	Define $\delta_s = \pi_s - \bar{\pi}$. Substituting $\pi_s = \bar{\pi} + \delta_s$:
79	\begin{equation}
80	M_{\text{global}}^{-1} M_s - I = \frac{\delta_s}{1-2\bar{\pi}} \begin{bmatrix} -1 & 1 \\ 1 & -1 \end{bmatrix}.
81	\end{equation}
82	
83	To verify: the $(1,1)$ entry of $M_{\text{global}}^{-1} M_s$ is
84	{\small
85	\begin{align}
86	&\frac{(1{-}\bar{\pi})(1{-}\bar{\pi}{-}\delta_s) - \bar{\pi}(\bar{\pi}{+}\delta_s)}{1-2\bar{\pi}} \nonumber\\
87	&= \frac{(1{-}\bar{\pi})^2 - (1{-}\bar{\pi})\delta_s - \bar{\pi}^2 - \bar{\pi}\delta_s}{1-2\bar{\pi}} \nonumber\\
88	&= \frac{1 - 2\bar{\pi} - \delta_s}{1-2\bar{\pi}} = 1 - \frac{\delta_s}{1-2\bar{\pi}}.
89	\end{align}
90	}
91	
92	Applying $(M_{\text{global}}^{-1} M_s - I)$ to $\boldsymbol{\tau}$:
93	{\small
94	\begin{align}
95	\text{bias}(z{=}0, s) &= \tfrac{\delta_s}{1-2\bar{\pi}}(-\tau(0) + \tau(1)) = +\tfrac{\delta_s \cdot \Delta\tau}{1-2\bar{\pi}}, \\
96	\text{bias}(z{=}1, s) &= \tfrac{\delta_s}{1-2\bar{\pi}}(\tau(0) - \tau(1)) = -\tfrac{\delta_s \cdot \Delta\tau}{1-2\bar{\pi}},
97	\end{align}
98	}
99	where $\Delta\tau = \tau(1) - \tau(0)$. \qed
100	
```
